### ⛔ A COLLISION MINTED BY THIS PROGRAMME — reported before the review
**"MCA" in this corpus means **Method Certification Assessment** — the artifact under review — used so in the M6 Critical Review, Authority Disposition §132, Consolidation §125, and Architecture Gate Review DD-8.**

**On 2026-07-30 I labelled the methodology-CHANGE assessment "MCA-R1" on the stated ground that *"no artifact of that name exists in the repository."*** ***THAT STATEMENT WAS FALSE*** — my search used "Methodology Change Assessment" and filename patterns `mca|methodology`, neither of which matches `Method_Certification_Assessment`.

**Consequences:** a **false claim published** (now corrected, superseded sentence retained) · a **PERMANENT collision minted** (identifier stability forbids renaming — bounded only: "MCA-R1" = the register-scoped change assessment; "MCA" unqualified = this artifact) · **the AFV backlog trigger is MET** (*one collision is a backlog item; a second makes it a candidate*).

> ***The sharpest fact: this collision was minted while the programme was actively recording the rule that names cannot be withdrawn. The party most alert to the cost of naming still incurred it — because the check was a HABIT rather than a STEP.***

**PMR-10 RAISED (not adopted): *before an identifier or acronym is minted, the corpus shall be searched for existing use of that token.*** **n=2, and both collisions produced a defect that ESCAPED** (AFV blocked an Authority recommendation; MCA published a false statement) — *so unlike PMR-1, the escape test is satisfied.* **Distinguished from the AFV backlog item: that is BACKWARD-looking normalization (by alias only); PMR-10 is FORWARD-looking prevention — and only the forward-looking rule changes future practice, which is why only it is a PMR.**

### VERDICT: INFERENCE DISCIPLINE EXEMPLARY — the strongest artifact in the programme on its own axis
**All ten commissioned boundary checks PASS** (one with MC-F1). Three properties no other artifact exhibits together:
1. **A MANDATORY T-2 declaration pre-scoping the whole document's vocabulary:** *"Every 'validated' below means same-lineage-validated on n=1 execution."* ***A qualifier that pre-scopes an entire document cannot be forgotten by a reader who starts at the top.***
2. **§9 records the outcomes it DECLINED and why** — *"Rejected alternative outcomes: Certified — overclaims n=1 same-lineage evidence."* ***An assessment that publishes the grade it refused to give makes its own restraint auditable.***
3. **§10 risk 3 names its own recommendation's failure mode: *"provisional-status ossification."***

### MC-F1 — MINOR
**§9's composite claims the two dimensions *"converge on the same label"* — but Method Design = PROVISIONALLY CERTIFIED while Operational Evidence = SUPPORTED BY ONE EXECUTION LINEAGE.** *One is a grade; the other is an evidence-standing statement.* **Remedy: say the dimensions are COMPATIBLE (the grade is bounded by the standing), not that they share a label. No grade changes.** ***The defect is in the sentence describing the relationship, not in the relationship.***

### ⭐ The candidate that DISSOLVED — and it matters more than the finding
**Candidate: *the composite inherits its STRONGEST component's confidence* — exactly PMR-2's defect class, which would have supplied PMR-2's MISSING SECOND DATUM and discharged a deferred candidate.**

⛔ **DISSOLVED: the two dimensions are not on a common scale, and the composite is bounded DOWNWARD (§9 refuses full certification and enumerates three unsupported claims).** ***A composite cannot inherit the stronger of two values that are not values of the same kind.***

> **PMR-2 gains NO second datum; its deferral stands undisturbed.**

***Recorded emphatically because the temptation ran the other way: confirming it would have discharged a deferred candidate, closed a CDR-R1 open item, and produced a Major finding. Verification before classification cost all three — PMR-9's candidate behaviour paying its way by SUBTRACTING a finding rather than finding a better one.***

### MC-O1 — Observation
**§6's component table rates the *Evidence Restatement stage* — which the designed methodology did not contain — as *"Validated."*** So the summary *"no component is rated insufficient"* counts a component the method lacked. **Observation: the row discloses its own status in its rating text and routes the consequence to an MCR.** *Same discipline OBS-CON-1 credited — honest signalling matters more than perfectly clean categories.*

## CDR-1 contract refinements applied — two checks RE-RUN, all four predicted risk areas PASS (2026-07-31)

**1. Governing question TIGHTENED (adopted, superseded wording retained):** ~~*"unsupported authority"*~~ → ***"unsupported DECISIONS"***. ***The authority EXISTS; the question is whether each EXERCISE of it is justified. "Unsupported authority" mis-locates the defect — a CDR cannot lack authority it was granted; it can only spend it without warrant.***

**2. Recognition boundary REFRAMED (adopted):** the review question is ***"did the Authority possess sufficient ADVISORY BASIS at this decision point?"*** — **not** whether the layer should exist or whether the recognition is true. **§4 had already answered the legitimacy question, not the truth question.** *Recorded because **a review that had answered the truth question would have looked identical from its verdict alone.***

**3. ⭐ CONFIGURATION CONTROL — broadened test, PASSES DECISIVELY.** Candidate dissolved on verification: **MCA §11 item 5 reads *"The staged certification-decision agenda carried from the baseline: freeze SDM v1 · freeze EOP v1 · adopt MCR-1 · declare Process Under Configuration Control — all CDR/Authority acts, none performed here."*** So the declaration is a **staged agenda item the MCA explicitly identifies as a CDR act it is not performing**, and *"carried from the baseline"* means **it predates the MCA as well**. **Not new governance.** *The Authority's caution — "declarative statements sometimes conceal new policy" — is exactly the right test, and this passes because the policy was staged, named and attributed before the decision point.*

**4. FREEZE-CONSISTENCY verified on its hardest candidate.** §4.1 commits *"nothing is created from scratch"*; **MCR-3's CDR text says *"The route is defined…"*** — which would be design content inside a decision record. ⛔ **Dissolved: MCA §148 already defines the route** (*"Refinement: a bounded, separately-commissioned…"*). ***"The route is defined" is a passive report of an existing definition, not an act of defining — and that distinction is verifiable only against the source, which is why it was checked rather than classified.***

**5. MCR-5's three sub-questions each answered YES:** the adopted half WAS recommended (MCA: *"Convergence-semantics **statement**"*) · the deferred half WAS left deferred (the MCA itself marks the instrument *"optional"*) · no third interpretation invented. ***Partial adoption is the easiest place for a decision record to drift into redesign, and this one drifted in neither direction: it neither promoted the optional half nor weakened the recommended one.***

### Outcome against the Authority's predicted ranking
**Predicted: (1) Recognition · (2) Configuration control · (3) MCR-5 · (4) Freeze wording.**
> **ALL FOUR VERIFIED. ALL FOUR PASS. No finding against the artifact in any of them.**

***The four highest-risk surfaces in a certification decision — recognizing, declaring, splitting, freezing — were each the places this record was most explicit about its basis.*** The Authority's expectation that authority/advisory and decision/execution separation would be its strongest aspects also holds.

**The review's one substantive result remains §6: a defect in MY OWN prior act (SDM v1.1's false authority claim about consolidation), not in the artifact under review.**

**State: FIFTEEN artifacts reviewed · CDR-1 complete, NO finding against the CDR · CDR-O1 open (observation) · CON-F2 open under M7-CF2 · register 10 candidates · v1.1's authority claim awaiting Authority decision on correction.**

## Session close — four-review cluster complete (2026-07-31)

**Authority determinations recorded in the Method:**

**1. VERIFICATION IS ITSELF A RESULT.** *"A review whose value is primarily verification rather than defect discovery is an entirely legitimate — and often desirable — outcome."* **CDR-1's zero findings are NOT a null result: each of the four surfaces most susceptible to overreach proved EXPLICITLY BOUNDED BY EARLIER GOVERNANCE.** ***That is POSITIVE evidence that an authority decision stayed within its constitutional role — a stronger statement than "no defects were found," which is compatible with not having looked.*** *Pairs with MC-1's exemplar: value is not the COUNT of findings (MC-1) and can be the ESTABLISHMENT OF A BOUND rather than discovery of a breach (CDR-1).*

**2. REVIEWS DO NOT ABSORB ADJACENT GOVERNANCE WORK.** ***Visibility is not jurisdiction.*** Evidence: CON-F2 routed to its own commission · CDR-1 §6's self-defect reported but NOT adjudicated · consolidation noted as authorized-but-uncommissioned with no commission issued. *The alternative — a review that fixes what it happens to see — produces artifacts whose scope is set by the accidents of what a reviewer noticed.*

**3. THE PROGRAMME'S DISCRIMINATING STANDARD.** Four reviews, four responsibilities, four tailored contracts: **ER-1 execution discipline · CON-1 stabilization discipline · MC-1 inference discipline · CDR-1 authority discipline.**
> **No longer *"is this document correct?"* but ***"did this document faithfully perform ITS SPECIFIC CONSTITUTIONAL ROLE?"***

***And this is stabilizer 3 observed from the inside: the CONTRACT varied while the FAMILY, the EMPHASIS COUNT and the RESPONSIBILITY held — which is why the taxonomy did not need to grow to accommodate four different object kinds.***

**MEMORY.md updated with a durable PKS governance block** (four-layer framework · stable taxonomy with the families⟂domains orthogonality · three stabilizers and their form · the standing rules as pointers · SDM v1.1 reference-defined baseline with extraction authorized-not-commissioned · certification state · **the two permanent acronym collisions**).

**2. Separation principle GENERALIZED (Authority formulation, stronger than any instance):**

*My "visibility is not jurisdiction" retained as its compressed form.* Three instances: CON-1 saw M7 implications and did not become an M7 review · CDR-1 saw the v1.1 authority issue and did not adjudicate it · CDR-1 saw extraction was authorized and did not commission it.

**`docs/implementation/PKS_IBC1_Recertification_Review.md`. Consequence family · AUTHORITY-IMPACT emphasis (AIA-1's existing slot — it traces earlier findings against later Authority acts). Count remains TEN; nothing minted.**

**The review's own header states: *"Object of review | IBC-1 Sections 1–13 **as presented inline in the review request**."*** **A repository search for IBC-1 returns exactly ONE file — the review itself. *IBC-1 has no artifact.***

|---|---|
| whether each finding's **GOVERNING BASIS** still holds (standards are all in the repo) | whether each finding still **APPLIES** to IBC-1 (*the text is gone*) |

> ***Every status is therefore CONDITIONAL on IBC-1's text being unchanged since 2026-07-28. None can be unconditional.***

**And the commission's five-status vocabulary (confirmed/superseded/dissolved/narrowed/strengthened) contains no term for *object absent*. GAP REPORTED, NOT FILLED — minting a status would breach the terminology discipline.**

- **IBC-C3 SUPERSEDED BY EXECUTION.** Its premise was a condition — *"until applications are made"* — now satisfied, so the contract's statement is now the accurate one. ***Retired as SUPERSEDED, not DISSOLVED: dissolved means the reasoning failed; superseded means the world moved. ### Boundary check 3 was ALREADY performed — by the review itself
**§7.1 (2026-07-28): *"0 architecture defects · 12 governance defects · 1 methodology observation · 6 improvement recommendations · 1 question"*** — *"nothing in IBC-1 asserts wrong architecture; every defect is a failure of TRANSMISSION."* **Re-certified; no finding changed category.** ***A finding set containing zero architecture defects cannot be invalidated by architectural change — which is why substantial baseline evolution retired only two of twenty-one.***

### ⛔ IBC-REC-F1 — MODERATE (against the REVIEW)
**§8.2 concludes *"the review is reproducible by anyone with repository access — which is the property Rule 16 exists to establish, and it holds."* THAT CLAIM IS FALSE, falsified by the review's own header.** A reader with repository access can verify every STANDARD and **no single FINDING**.

> ***Citing a governed standard makes a finding AUDITABLE in its REASONING and leaves it UNREPRODUCIBLE in its APPLICATION — and §8.2 collapsed the two.***

**Remedy: annotation (frozen historical evidence). Credit recorded: §8.2 came closer than anything in the corpus — it found *"only 6 of 21 findings are artifact-local"* and drew a real consequence for Section 11. It stopped one step short of asking whether the artifact was available at all.**

### Observation, NOT absorbed
**IBC-M4 (2026-07-28) and CON-F2 (2026-07-31) are THE SAME UNDERLYING FACT at two different chain points, found three days apart by reviews that did not know of each other: the three contested memberships (Risk · Question · Exception record) are not being carried forward.** **CON-F2 is open under M7-CF2 and NOT adjudicated here** — *a review's scope is determined by its commission, not by everything it can observe.*

### VERDICT
**The review REMAINS CONSTITUTIONALLY CORRECT** — authority boundary, decision traceability, DDD boundary (it redesigns nothing), scope and terminology discipline all PASS.

> **MOST CONSEQUENTIAL RECOMMENDATION, recorded not commissioned: before IBC-1 is revised, corrected or conformance-assessed, IBC-1 must EXIST as a governed artifact with the provenance apparatus IBC-S1 names.** ***Every remediation of a 21-finding review presupposes a text to remediate, and there is none.***

**State: SIXTEEN artifacts reviewed · IBC-REC-F1 open · CDR-O1 open · CON-F2 open under M7-CF2.**

## IBC-1 re-certification — two Authority refinements applied (2026-07-31)

### 1. IBC-REC-F1 RESTATED as a CONFLATION of two reproducibility properties
**Authority refinement adopted in place of my narrower framing.** The defect is **not** merely that the artifact is absent — it is that §8.2 **conflates**:
- **REASONING reproducibility** — *another reviewer can inspect every governing source and understand **why** the conclusions were reached* → ✅ **ESTABLISHED, genuinely**
- **APPLICATION reproducibility** — *another reviewer can inspect the **reviewed artifact** and independently determine whether the conclusions still follow* → ⛔ **NOT established, and not establishable from the repository**

> ***The origin taxonomy is sound; the inference treats reasoning reproducibility as if it ENTAILED application reproducibility. It does not — a finding can be fully explicable and wholly uncheckable at the same time.***

**Superseded framing retained:** ~~*"the problem is that the object is absent"*~~ — **true but narrower: it describes THIS case rather than the property that failed, and a rule drawn from it would not generalize to an object that is PRESENT BUT ALTERED.**

### 2. Recommendation SOFTENED to the Authority's procedural phrasing
> **No remediation or conformance assessment of IBC-1 can be COMPLETED unless there is a governed instance of IBC-1 against which those activities are performed.**

*Replaces* ~~*"IBC-1 must EXIST as a governed artifact"*~~. ***The softer form states a PREREQUISITE without implying that creating the artifact is itself commissioned work — which the stronger form did by implication, and which no act has authorized.*** The underlying fact is unchanged.

### 3. The four-step sequence recorded as an OBSERVATION — and NO candidate raised
**(1) verify the OBJECT EXISTS → (2) verify governing SOURCES → (3) verify APPLICABILITY → (4) only then CLASSIFY.**

***Step 1 was decisive exactly once: the IBC-1 re-certification could not have reached its result by starting at step 2, because the object's absence is invisible from the governing sources.***

**⚠️ Recorded as an OBSERVATION and deliberately NOT raised as a methodology candidate: the Authority stated it DESCRIPTIVELY — *"that ordering reflects the same discipline that has emerged"* — and did not propose it as a rule. Under Amendment 4 that is RECOGNITION, which is neither adoption nor even a proposal.** *Were it prescriptive it would require PMR → MCA → CDR and would be DISTINCT from PMR-9 (which presupposes an object). Relationship noted for a future assessment; no candidate minted.*

**Register unchanged at 10 candidates — no inflation from this review.**

**State: SIXTEEN artifacts reviewed · IBC-REC-F1 open · CDR-O1 open · CON-F2 open under M7-CF2 · register 10.**

## IBC-REC-F1 + CDR-O1 DISPOSED — ACCEPT both (Authority act, 2026-07-31)

**IBC-REC-F1 — ACCEPT · remedy ANNOTATION**, placed adjacent to §8.2's conclusion where a reader meets the false claim. **§8.2's origin taxonomy is NOT altered — it is sound; only the inference drawn from it failed.** Operationalized in the Authority's terms — the two questions a later reviewer actually asks:
- ***"Why did the reviewer reach this conclusion?"*** → ✅ answerable (reasoning reproducibility holds)
- ***"Would I reach the same conclusion from the reviewed artifact?"*** → ⛔ not answerable (application reproducibility does not)

**CDR-O1 — ACCEPT as an observation; CARRIED, not remediated.** CDR-1 closes with **no finding against its artifact** and one carried observation.

**Nothing else changed: IBC-1 not created · CDR not amended · CON-F2 stays with M7-CF2 · certification and governance state unchanged · no candidate raised by either disposition.**

### ⭐ PRECONDITION OF REVIEWABILITY — recorded as the most fundamental result
> **A review is reproducible only if BOTH its governing sources AND its reviewed object remain available. Governing sources alone are insufficient. The reviewed object alone is insufficient.**

**Recorded as a statement of FACT about review evidence, not a prescription about conduct — which is why it raises no methodology candidate.**

**Structural split restated in the Authority's stronger wording** *(superseded form retained: ~~"the governing basis is re-certifiable; the reviewed object is not"~~ — a repository fact)*:
> **The review's NORMATIVE BASIS remains independently verifiable, while its APPLICATION EVIDENCE is no longer independently reproducible.**

*The stronger form aligns the structural split with the reproducibility distinction that explains it, instead of leaving them as two separate observations about one failure.*

### ⭐ The subtle error the ordering prevented — named by the Authority
> ***Treating the continued existence of the REVIEW DOCUMENT as evidence that the REVIEWED OBJECT was still available.***

**Invisible from the governing sources: a reviewer starting there would have verified twenty-one standards, found nineteen intact, and published a confident re-certification of findings checkable against nothing.** ***Evidence governs classification — not continuity of documentation. A surviving review is evidence that a review happened, and evidence of nothing else.***

### The missing status — admission condition recorded, still unminted
**Authority determination: *if a future methodology commission concludes "object unavailable" deserves first-class status, it can introduce one with evidence from MULTIPLE CASES rather than this single instance.*** ***That is the admission ladder applied to a STATUS rather than a control — and the same threshold: one case is an observation, repetition is a candidate.***

**State: SIXTEEN artifacts reviewed · no review open · no finding open · CON-F2 remains under M7-CF2 (not executed) · register 10 candidates.**

## AFV-F4 premise verification — two refinements applied · Authority acts recorded as governed artifacts (2026-07-31)

### 1. REJECT-side association WITHDRAWN as imprecise (Authority refinement)
**Superseded, retained:** ~~*"closer to the REJECT-side breadth the commission's own asymmetry warning exists to prevent"*~~
**Operative:**

As such it EXCEEDS THE SCOPE of AFV-F4 and requires independent Authority consideration.**

***My phrasing conflated two axes: breadth of constitutional implication vs choice among ACCEPT/REJECT/DEFER. A rationale can exceed a finding's scope while supporting ANY of the three outcomes — the analysis belongs on SCOPE EXPANSION, not on one disposition side.***

### 2. ER-F1 precision recorded
**The realization is an OBSERVATION ABOUT GOVERNANCE PROCESS, NOT evidence that ER-F1 was mis-classified.** ***A severity is assigned on the evidence available at assessment; later realization is a datum about the process, not a correction of the rating.*** *A risk occurring once does not retroactively re-rank the finding that named it.*

### 3. ⭐ AUTHORITY ACTS ARE GOVERNED ARTIFACTS IN THEIR OWN RIGHT (Integrity Model)
> **An Authority act is a governed artifact, with UNIQUENESS, TRACEABILITY and CHANGE CONTROL preserved across its lifecycle.**

| Property | What it forbids |
|---|---|
| **Uniqueness** | a second disposition of one finding — *nothing would establish which governs* |
| **Traceability** | re-deciding behind a promotion — *the act that gated a correction explains why it executed* |
| **Change control** | ***altering an act's RATIONALE while preserving its outcome — two Authority acts may reach the same operational outcome on different constitutional grounds, and changing the grounds is itself a governance change*** |

**The repeatable discipline recorded as an OBSERVATION, not a prescription:** (1) verify the commission premise still exists · (2) determine whether the requested Authority act has ALREADY occurred · (3) refuse to duplicate a discharged constitutional act · (4) separate genuinely new governance questions into their own commissions.

***Higher than issuing a correct decision: it treats the decision RECORD as something a well-intentioned duplicate can corrupt.*** **Related to the review-side sequence in the Method (object → sources → applicability → classify) — both begin by verifying that the thing being acted on exists. Neither minted as a rule; both stated descriptively. Register unchanged at 10.**

## Two final refinements (2026-07-31)

### 1. "A surviving review is evidence of nothing else" — WITHDRAWN as too absolute
**Superseded, retained:** ~~*"A surviving review is evidence that a review happened, and evidence of nothing else."*~~
**Operative (Authority formulation):**
> **A surviving review IS evidence that the review occurred and records the reasoning applied at that time. It is NOT, by itself, evidence that the reviewed object remains available, or that the review's conclusions remain independently reproducible.**

***My version understated the evidentiary value of historical review records — and self-defeatingly so: a surviving review is also evidence of what was reviewed, what was concluded, and what reasoning was recorded, which is precisely why 19 of 21 governing bases could be re-certified at all. The absolute form would have made the IBC-1 re-certification impossible to justify.***

### 2. ⭐ A SECOND VOCABULARY GAP, of the same shape
**The AFV-F4 commission offered ACCEPT · REJECT · DEFER. The correct outcome was none of them:**
> **No disposition is constitutionally available, because the requested Authority act has already been performed.**

| Vocabulary | Gap | Shape |
|---|---|---|
| **Review statuses** (confirmed/superseded/dissolved/narrowed/strengthened) | no term for **object absent** | *presumes the object exists* |
| **Disposition outcomes** (accept/reject/defer) | no term for **act already performed** | *presumes a decision is still available* |

> ***Both vocabularies presume the act they describe is POSSIBLE. Neither can express the finding that it is not.***

**Both REPORTED, NEITHER minted, on the same admission condition — evidence from multiple cases, not a single instance.** ***And the symmetry is itself one datum: two gaps of one shape in two days is a pattern worth watching, not a pattern established.***

### 3. Evidentiary basis stated plainly, per the Authority's confidence separation
**Every factual claim in the premise verification — the disposition's existence and decision, the packages' execution, the artifacts' promotion — is QUOTED DIRECTLY from the repository, not inferred.** ***The governance reasoning is mine; the facts are the record's.***

**FINAL STATE (2026-07-31): SIXTEEN artifacts reviewed · no review open · no finding open · no disposition pending · register 10 candidates · SDM v1.1 / EOP v1.1 operative · Operational Evidence correctly at zero.**

## Closing record — three Authority contributions, one bound (2026-07-31)

**1. The four evidentiary questions (Method)** — the operational form of the surviving-review principle: *what reasoning was used?* ✅ · *what conclusions were reached?* ✅ · *does the reviewed object still exist?* ⛔ not by itself · *are the conclusions still independently reproducible?* ⛔ not by itself. ***Four distinct questions where my absolute formulation collapsed them into one; the first two rows are why 19 of 21 bases could be re-certified.***

**2. Why the evidence/reasoning split matters (Method):**
> **Evidence should be independently VERIFIABLE. Reasoning should be independently REVIEWABLE.**

***Separate, a later auditor can disagree with the reasoning without disputing the evidence, or dispute the evidence without discarding the reasoning. Collapsed, a challenge to either becomes a challenge to both.***

**3. ⭐ AVAILABILITY OF AUTHORITY IS A PRECONDITION FOR ITS EXERCISE (Integrity Model):**
> **There is no live constitutional act to perform where the requested Authority act has already been performed.**

*The AFV-F4 commission offered ACCEPT · REJECT · DEFER and the actual state was ORTHOGONAL to all three.* ***Not merely a missing vocabulary term: availability of authority is itself a precondition for exercising authority.***

**⚠️ THE BOUND, recorded per the Authority's caution — and it uses the programme's own principle/control distinction:**

| RECORDED | NOT RECORDED |
|---|---|
| **the PRINCIPLE** — availability precedes exercise | **a mandatory AVAILABILITY CHECK on every governance action** |
| *a principle describes; recording it costs nothing* | ***a control mandates activity, and mandating one requires evidence its absence let defects escape*** |

**Evidence: TWO cases (IBC-1 — object absent; AFV-F4 — act already performed). Enough to record the observation; NOT enough to require a formally modelled availability check.** ***The direction the evidence points is not yet the direction it establishes.***

**4. THE PROGRESSION OF QUESTIONS (Integrity Model):** early — *is the artifact correct?* · intermediate — *is the REVIEW correct?* · current — *is the constitutional ACT still AVAILABLE?* ***The third is qualitatively different: the process now evaluates the VALIDITY OF THE GOVERNANCE ACTIONS REQUESTED OF IT.***

**⚠️ A SEPARATE AXIS from the four governance maturities — those classify what is GOVERNED; this classifies what is ASKED. NOT a fifth maturity, and not evidence the methodology is finished: open candidates and operational evidence remain.** *What it shows is a process able to decline work for CONSTITUTIONAL reasons supported by evidence rather than on procedural rules or preference.*

| Case | Finding | Outcome |
|---|---|---|

> **Dissolve CON-F2 not because later governance acts OCCURRED, but because after those acts the specific constitutional issue CON-F2 identified CAN NO LONGER BE DEMONSTRATED.**

## ⛔ M7-CF2 SELF-CORRECTION — "strengthened" was wrong, and so was the CLASS (2026-07-31)

**The Authority withheld the disposition pending the evidence detail and asked whether it supports *"strengthened"* rather than *"confirmed"*. It does not. BOTH my classifications were wrong.**

### 1. CONFIRMED, not STRENGTHENED — the evidence base did not grow
**Evidence when raised: Risk 0 · Exception 0 · Question 0 · §3 cites the §4 package. Evidence now: *identical* — the same three counts, the same citation, the same greps.**

> ***"Strengthened" requires MORE EVIDENCE FOR THE CLAIM. What actually happened is that a MITIGATING CONSIDERATION was WITHDRAWN. Those are not the same thing, and conflating them inflates a finding without adding a fact.***

**Contrast with a genuine strengthening already on the record: IBC-M1 strengthened because the omitted-baseline list literally GREW. Here nothing grew.**

### 2. ⭐ THE FINDING CHANGES CLASS — the Authority's third option is correct
| As recorded | Now |
|---|---|
| **STAGING-CONSEQUENCE finding** — *"M7 execution followed one side of an incompatible package"*; subject = the relation between Consolidation §4 and M7 | **INTERNAL-CONSISTENCY finding IN M7** — *M7 has a convention for non-disposed participants, applies it to the unpartitioned core, and does not apply it to the contested memberships*; **subject = M7 alone** |

> ***Once the staging contradiction dissolves, the finding no longer depends on the consolidation at all — it is an inconsistency in M7's application of its OWN convention, and it would stand even if §4 had never existed.***

**Consequences, not cosmetic:** ownership is **wholly M7's** (the consolidation is no longer implicated; CON-F1's disposition stands and its subject is now unrelated) · the remedy shifts from *"resolve a staging contradiction"* to *"apply an existing convention consistently"* — smaller and better-defined · **the characterization question sharpens: *"incomplete" becomes the more natural reading and "lawful" the harder one, because the standard is now M7's OWN practice rather than an external obligation.***

### The evidence, as requested
1. **Convention adopted:** §2 *"the unpartitioned core's participation (**recorded, unclassified**)"*; §6 legend `dashed ╌` seam · `dotted ·` outer edge · `~~` unpartitioned region · `⚠` uncertainty; §4 participants include the CBC-3 candidate seam.
2. **Relation to §4-4 / §4-7:** it **satisfies** §4-4 and **falsifies** §4-7's exclusive reading.
3. **Does it leave the contradiction unresolved?** **No — it RESOLVES it.** CON-F1's premise fails.
4. **Does it reveal a different inconsistency?** **YES — internal to M7.** *That is the class change.*

***Recorded plainly: the Authority declined to accept "strengthened" without the evidence, the evidence did not support it, and the classification is corrected DOWNWARD on severity-language and SIDEWAYS on class — the sideways move being the finding that matters.***

**STATE: CON-F2 — CASE B, CONFIRMED on unchanged evidence, RECLASSIFIED as an internal-consistency finding in M7. Awaiting Authority characterization (incomplete / lawful) and, if warranted, a change-control act against promoted M7.**

## The CLASSIFICATION LADDER adopted — and it immediately overturned one of my earlier classifications (2026-07-31)

**Note: the confirmed-vs-strengthened correction for CON-F2 was already made in the preceding act — classified CONFIRMED on the grounds the Authority states (identical greps, identical citation; a MITIGATION was withdrawn, not evidence added).**

### The ladder, adopted and recorded in the Method
| Status | Requires |
|---|---|
| **DISSOLVED** | the original proposition is NO LONGER TRUE |
| **CONFIRMED** | the original proposition REMAINS TRUE |
| **STRENGTHENED** | remains true **AND NEW INDEPENDENT EVIDENCE** supports it more strongly than when raised |

> ***The third step is the discriminator. Without requiring NEW INDEPENDENT EVIDENCE, "confirmed" and "strengthened" become indistinguishable — and every surviving finding drifts upward on re-examination.***

**Two failure modes it prevents, BOTH observed here:** treating the **withdrawal of a mitigation** as strengthening (CON-F2) · treating a **cross-case pattern** as support for one case (IBC-M4).

### ⛔ AUDIT of every "strengthened" in the IBC-1 re-certification — one OVERTURNED
| Finding | New independent evidence? | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| **IBC-M1** (baseline omits artifacts) | ✅ the baseline GREW — seven more artifacts are now omitted | **STANDS** |
| **IBC-M3** ("certified" conflates statuses) | ✅ a THIRD status now exists (PROMOTED) | **STANDS** |
| **IBC-M4** (memberships never named in IBC-1) | ⛔ **NO** — CON-F2 is evidence about **M7's** content, not IBC-1's | ⛔ **CORRECTED to CONFIRMED** |

***The ladder earned its place immediately: applied to prior work it upheld two classifications and overturned one — and the one it overturned failed for the same reason as CON-F2, which is why a LADDER is better than a judgment.***

### RESTATE THE ORIGINAL CLAIM FIRST — recorded with what it prevents
**(1) restate the original claim → (2) verify the current object → (3) compare claim and evidence → (4) only then classify.**

> ***What it avoids: SILENTLY CHANGING THE PROPOSITION UNDER REVIEW. Many review processes drift into testing a different question than the original one — and a drifted review can be entirely rigorous about the wrong claim.***

**Recorded as an OBSERVED pattern, not admitted as a rule.**

**STATE: CON-F2 — CASE B, CONFIRMED, reclassified as an internal-consistency finding in M7. IBC-M4 corrected to CONFIRMED. Awaiting Authority characterization of M7 (incomplete / lawful).**

## ⛔ SECOND SELF-CORRECTION on M7-CF2 — the "reclassification" was a SUBSTITUTION (2026-07-31)

**The Authority applied the programme's own discipline to my ontology change: *"changing a finding's STATUS is different from changing its ONTOLOGY… treat ontology changes with the same discipline you've been applying to status changes."* Applied — and the reclassification does not survive it.**

### CON-F2's proposition had TWO limbs; the dissolution test falsified BOTH
| Limb | Standing |
|---|---|
| **(a)** the consolidation staged **MUTUALLY INCOMPATIBLE** obligations | ⛔ **FALSIFIED** — §4-4 and §4-7 are compatible; M7's *recorded, unclassified* convention satisfies both |
| **(b)** M7 execution **FOLLOWED ONE SIDE** of that package | ⛔ **FALSIFIED BY DEPENDENCY** — *"followed one side" presupposes two sides; with one coherent obligation set the description does not apply* |

### What survived was NOT CON-F2's proposition
**The surviving fact is framed in terms of M7's INTERNAL PRACTICE; CON-F2's proposition was framed in terms of the STAGING RELATIONSHIP.**

> ***Different propositions. Calling the move a "class change" implied the proposition persisted in a new category. It did not: I SUBSTITUTED a new proposition for the original — precisely "silently changing the proposition under review", the failure mode I had recorded as one to prevent IN THE SAME ACT.***

### Corrected disposition — the Authority's THIRD option, not the second
*(Their option set was: strengthened · changes class · **a different finding replaces it**. I picked the second; the third is correct.)*
- **CON-F2 (proposition as stated): DISSOLVED** — the incompatibility premise is falsified and was never real; *"apparent" was the right word in CON-F1's annotation.*
- **M7-F1 (NEW finding)** carries the surviving observation, with **its OWN identifier** — ***a finding that inherits an identifier inherits a proposition.***
- **My CASE assignment was also wrong: CASE A for CON-F2 as stated + one new finding. Not Case B.**

### M7-F1 — NEW, reported not disposed
**M7 possesses a governed convention for non-disposed participants (§2 *"recorded, unclassified"*; §6 `~~` / `dashed ╌` / `dotted ·` / `⚠`), applies it to the unpartitioned core and the CBC-3 candidate seam, and does NOT apply it to the three contested memberships.**
**Class: internal consistency, M7 alone** — *it stands whether or not Consolidation §4 ever existed, and that independence is what distinguishes it from CON-F2 rather than continuing it.* **Evidence: Risk 0 · Exception 0 · Question 0 · §3 cites the staging package · the convention is in use elsewhere in the same document.** **Classification: NEW — a new finding has no prior status to carry.** **M7 NOT amended; no change item raised.**
**Recommended characterization: INCOMPLETE** (*Authority's reasoning adopted: "internally consistent where applied, but not yet complete in its application" — narrower than constitutionally defective, more evidence-based than entirely lawful*). **The Authority decides.**

### Method updated — ONTOLOGY discipline
> **Changing a finding's STATUS differs in kind from changing its ONTOLOGY. An ontology change is the BROADER claim and must be justified explicitly, never assumed.**

**The decisive test: *does the ORIGINAL PROPOSITION survive, merely categorized differently?* If yes → a genuine class change. If no → ***a SUBSTITUTION: a new proposition wearing the old finding's identifier.***

> ***A finding that inherits an identifier inherits a proposition. Where the proposition does not survive, the identifier must not travel.***

***Three corrections in one examination — "strengthened"→"confirmed", "class change"→"substitution", "Case B"→"Case A + new finding" — each caught by the Authority applying a criterion the programme had already recorded, and NONE requiring new evidence: the same three zero-counts supported all three readings, which is exactly why the criteria matter more than the evidence here.***

**STATE: CON-F2 DISSOLVED (proposition falsified) · M7-F1 NEW and open, recommended characterization INCOMPLETE · IBC-M4 corrected to CONFIRMED · awaiting Authority disposition of M7-F1.**

### ⭐ The governing principle (Integrity Model)
> **The PROPOSITION is primary. The IDENTIFIER is a REFERENCE to that proposition — not the other way around. Where the two diverge, the identifier retires WITH its proposition rather than carrying a falsified premise forward under a familiar name.**

### The auditability test that settled Option B
**The Authority's argument, recorded as the operative test for any future identifier-continuity question:** *"Option B keeps the identifier but annotates away half of its meaning… CON-F2 means… except not the original first limb… but the second limb… except that the second depended on the first… except that we've reclassified it."*

> ***The annotation preserves HISTORY but no longer preserves SEMANTIC CLARITY. Those are different properties, and an identifier whose meaning requires a chain of nested exceptions has stopped functioning as a reference.***

**⚠️ Recorded so the two identifier rules are not read as conflicting: identifier STABILITY forbids REASSIGNING an identifier to different content (clarify → alias → annotate, never renumber); this principle governs whether an identifier TRAVELS with a changed proposition.** ***Never renumber — but do not stretch either. Retire and raise anew.***

### Established preference, n = 4 independent instances
*superseded vs dissolved · AFV-F4 (the constitutional act had already occurred) · IBC-REC-F1 (reasoning vs application reproducibility) · CON-F2 → M7-F1.*
> ***Where the underlying PROPOSITION changes, the programme creates a CLEARER SEMANTIC BOUNDARY rather than stretching an existing identifier.***

**Recorded as an OBSERVED PREFERENCE, not admitted as a rule** — *stated prescriptively it would govern future classification conduct and require PMR → MCA → CDR. Its n=4 exceeds most register entries, which is a fact about the evidence, not a reason to skip the path.*

## Dissolution stated IN FULL · three kinds of change consolidated (2026-07-31)

### The dissolution wording — a bare "DISSOLVED" MISLEADS
**Authority refinement adopted verbatim, applied at M7-CF2 §3E and in CON-1's §11 annotation:**
> **CON-F2 — DISSOLVED because its original constitutional proposition has been FALSIFIED by later verification. This dissolution does NOT imply that no governance concern remains. A distinct concern regarding INCOMPLETE APPLICATION of M7's established convention has been recorded separately as M7-F1.**

| A bare "DISSOLVED" says | The full statement says |
|---|---|
| the finding is retired | **the original PROPOSITION retired · a DIFFERENT proposition emerged · and where to find it** |

> ***General form recorded: where a finding is dissolved but its underlying OBSERVATION survives under a new identifier, the dissolution MUST carry a pointer to the successor. A dissolution without one reads as an ALL-CLEAR — and an all-clear is exactly what the evidence does not support.***

### ⭐ THREE KINDS OF CHANGE consolidated (Integrity Model)
| Change | What changes | Discipline | Instances |
|---|---|---|---|
| **STATUS** | how strongly the same proposition is held | **the classification ladder** (*strengthened needs NEW INDEPENDENT EVIDENCE*) | CON-F2 · IBC-M4 (both corrected) · IBC-M1 · IBC-M3 |
| **RATIONALE** | the GROUNDS for the same outcome | ***a rationale change is itself a governance change*** — it may not be folded into a re-decision | **AFV-F4** (repository-scope grounds vs the model's own contingency) · **CDR-1 §6** (v1.1's form right, stated reason false) |
| **ONTOLOGY** | **WHAT THE FINDING IS ABOUT** | **a NEW identifier** — *test: does the original proposition survive, merely categorized differently? If no → SUBSTITUTION* | **CON-F2 → M7-F1** |

> ***The three are ordered by breadth, and each is easy to mistake for the one above it. A SUBSTITUTION presented as a STATUS CHANGE is the most dangerous, because it looks like housekeeping.***

*The Authority's dependency observation is what made this legible: limb (b) was falsified BY DEPENDENCY on limb (a), so the finding was dismantled at the level of its LOGICAL STRUCTURE, not merely re-graded — and the surviving observation derives from neither limb.*

## Causality reversed on the ontology row · FOUR concepts recorded (2026-07-31)

### 1. ⚠️ Causality stated in the correct direction (Authority refinement)
**Superseded:** ~~*"Ontology → a new identifier"*~~ — **inverted the order.**
**Operative:** ***the ONTOLOGY CHANGE is the conceptual event; the new identifier is a GOVERNANCE CONSEQUENCE that NORMALLY FOLLOWS.***

**Why the hedge *"normally"* is load-bearing: a proposition can change without any successor being warranted — where the observation does not survive at all, the finding simply dissolves and nothing follows it.** *My original phrasing made the identifier definitional of the change, which would have forced a successor in every case.*

### 2. ⭐ THE FOUR CONCEPTS (Integrity Model) — the axis that makes the three-tier model coherent
| # | Concept | Question |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | **EVIDENCE** | what is OBSERVED |
| **2** | **PROPOSITION** | what is CLAIMED |
| **3** | **RATIONALE** | why the claim is JUSTIFIED |
| **4** | **DISPOSITION** | what governance DECIDES to do |

**The three changes operate on the middle two; evidence is the base, disposition the output:** **status** = the strength with which **2** is held · **rationale** = **3**, leaving **2** and **4** intact · **ontology** = **2** itself, which is why **4** must be retaken and an identifier normally follows.

> ***These four are routinely conflated, and EVERY conflation this programme has caught was a collapse between TWO ADJACENT LEVELS: strengthened-vs-confirmed collapsed 1 into 2 · SDM v1.1's authority claim collapsed 3 into 4 · CON-F2's "reclassification" collapsed 2 into a status change.***

**The CON-F2 resolution respects all four: the EVIDENCE was re-examined · the original PROPOSITION shown false · a new PROPOSITION identified · the DISPOSITION preserved both through dissolution plus a successor pointer.**

### M7-F1's scope, confirmed as the Authority states it
**An INCOMPLETENESS finding concerning application of M7's established convention.** *Not a constitutional contradiction · not a consolidation defect · not a methodological issue.* **Whether it leads to change control is an AUTHORITY question, not something embedded in the review.**

**FINAL STATE 2026-07-31: SIXTEEN artifacts reviewed · CON-F2 DISSOLVED with successor pointer · M7-F1 RAISED and open (scope: incompleteness) · M7 unmodified · no other finding open · register 10 · SDM v1.1 / EOP v1.1 operative · Operational Evidence correctly at zero.**

**Superseded:** ~~*"ontology = the proposition itself, which is why the disposition must be retaken"*~~
**Operative (Authority formulation):**
> **Ontology changes ALTER THE GOVERNING PROPOSITION. Where governance action DEPENDS on that proposition, a new governance decision or finding will NORMALLY be required.**

**Counter-cases the universal form would have mis-stated:** a review finding may dissolve and be replaced **without an immediate Authority disposition** · an observation may change ontology and **never become a governance decision** · a methodology candidate may split **without any Authority act**.

*In the CON-F2 case a disposition WAS retaken — but that was CONTINGENT on governance action depending on the proposition, not NECESSARY from the ontology change itself. My phrasing generalized a contingent fact into a rule.*

> **A PROMISING EXPLANATORY MODEL, not a universal rule.**

*It explains three cases from this programme (1→2 strengthened-vs-confirmed · 3→4 SDM v1.1's authority claim · 2→status CON-F2's "reclassification").* **It becomes a stronger methodological claim only if it continues to explain FUTURE cases — and it is falsifiable in the obvious way: a conflation between NON-ADJACENT levels would refute it.**

***Held on the same terms as the three stabilizers: derived from this programme's evidence, revisable, with withdrawal as its lawful exit.***

**Five instances:** the three stabilizers (observed, then bounded as revisable principles) · PMR-8/9/10 (routed, not adopted) · confirmed-vs-strengthened (*strengthened needs NEW INDEPENDENT EVIDENCE*) · status/rationale/ontology (separated, then the ontology→disposition entailment softened to a dependency) · the adjacent-level model (*promising, not universal*, with an explicit refutation condition).

> **Whenever a useful explanatory model emerges, keep treating it as PROVISIONAL until it demonstrates predictive value across additional cases.**

***The failure mode it guards against is not error but PREMATURE PERMANENCE: a model adopted on its explanatory fit becomes unfalsifiable the moment it is treated as settled, because every later case is then read through it.***

## 🏁 M7-F1 DISPOSED — ACCEPT, EXTEND CONVENTION (Authority act, 2026-07-31)

**`docs/implementation/M7_F1_Authority_Disposition.md`.** Premise verified LIVE — unlike the AFV-F4 commission, this finding was genuinely undisposed.

### ⚠️ EVIDENCE VERIFICATION BEFORE DECIDING found ONE stated row FALSE
**The commission asserted: *"M7 has an established convention for representing **contested memberships** — ✅ Confirmed."*** ⛔ **Not true of M7.** Its convention is for **NON-DISPOSED PARTICIPANTS at the level of REGIONS and SEAMS** (§2: *"the CBC-3 candidate seam; the unpartitioned core's participation (recorded, unclassified)"*; §6: `dashed`/`~~`/`⚠`). **The word *"contested"* appears in M7 ZERO times, and its in-scope list does not mention them.**

> ***The correction exposed the normative step the framing concealed: extending the convention to INDIVIDUAL CONTESTED MEMBERS is a transfer ACROSS OBJECT KINDS. A region is a bounded area whose interior is undecided; a contested membership is a single element whose home is disputed. That a notation serves the first does not establish it serves the second — and that gap IS the "ought" the Authority must decide rather than inherit from the framing.***

*The commission-design refinement worked exactly as intended: an embedded factual assertion would have become an Authority fact by being acted on. Catching it made the decision HARDER, not easier — which is the point.*

### DECISION: ACCEPT — EXTEND CONVENTION, on CONSTITUTIONAL ground
> **Not because consistency requires it — consistency across object kinds is precisely what the evidence does NOT establish — but because the CONSTITUTIONAL RULE AGAINST IMPLICIT DECISION requires it.**

**Reasoning:** the three are *recorded, unassigned* in the governed baseline · ***a contested element absent from the strategic model is indistinguishable, to a later reader, from one that was never contested*** · that indistinguishability **IS resolution by omission** · so **extension DECIDES NOTHING — it records that a question remains open, which is the opposite of resolving it.**

**Asymmetry acknowledged: ACCEPT—Extend is the NARROW act.** ***ACCEPT—Intentional Incompleteness would be the BROADER act, because it asserts the omission was CHOSEN, and no record shows that it was.*** REJECT would leave a contested matter invisible in the authoritative relationship model.

**Object-kind gap addressed rather than waved away: M7's `⚠ uncertainty marker` is already NOT region-bound, so the transfer is smaller than §2's gap suggested — but it remains a transfer, and it is DECIDED here rather than assumed.**

### NOT decided (explicit)
**placement — Risk · Question · Exception record remain UNASSIGNED** · CBC-3 status unchanged · promotion unchanged · OQ-PKS-7 and all open questions unchanged · **whether the convention transfers to other object kinds GENERALLY — no general rule established** · Consolidation §3's constitutional check still reserved · **no methodology change, no PMR raised** · **M7's text UNTOUCHED.**

### Next step — one act, separately commissioned
**A change-control commission against promoted M7**: these three elements only · **visibility WITHOUT placement** (*an implementation that assigned them would exceed this disposition*) · a change-control act with its own record, **not** an editorial fold. **No further planning or verification layer is required, and none should be created.**

| **Execution** | performs **ONLY** that commissioned mechanism |

**The filter requires evidence that a control's absence let defects ESCAPE. The register's counts do not record which instances escaped the stage that owned the check:**

- **PMR-10** — the **AFV** collision **blocked an Authority recommendation**; the **MCA** collision **published a false claim**. *Both escaped the acts that caused them.*

***The filter's wording turns on exactly this distinction, and no act has yet made it. MCA-R2 must, as a reading of the existing filter and NOT as a new rule.***

### Constraints written into the commission
**Circularity resolved** — evaluate as HYPOTHESES under the existing certified methodology; ***MCA-R2 may INTERPRET the filter but may NOT apply PMR-8 to PMR-8*** (per CDR-R1 §1's interpret-vs-mandate boundary). **⚠️ Residual carried: the ASSESSMENT can be procedure-neutral while the EVIDENCE cannot — all three candidates' evidence was gathered in acts that already applied the proposed ordering.** **The filter's blind spot (it measures only defect prevention) carried forward unresolved.** **Three interactions recorded** (PMR-8⟂PMR-9 deliberately unbundled per CDR-R1's PMR-7 lesson · PMR-9⟂RET-3 assertion-vs-classification · PMR-10⟂the AFV backlog, forward vs backward looking).

**2. ⭐ DIRECT vs DERIVED traceability recorded (Integrity Model) as a governance insight:** *both kinds of implementation wording are LAWFUL and differ in their traceability PATH, not their legitimacy* — **DIRECT** from the Authority disposition · **DERIVED** from the commissioned implementation design constrained by existing governance. ***An execution report claiming "all traceable" without distinguishing the two asserts more than it verified — and conceals the one place where implementation judgment was exercised.*** *Consequence: state the path per element; derived wording that cannot be traced through the commissioned design to the disposition is scope creep, and only the distinction makes that testable.*

## ⛔ REPOSITORY-SCOPE DETERMINATION COMMISSIONED — and evidence verification changed the act entirely (2026-07-31)

**`docs/implementation/PKS_Repository_Scope_Determination_Commission.md`. Authority priority ⭐1, adopted: decide the boundary BEFORE reorganizing to reflect it. NOT EXECUTED.**

### ⛔ THE ORIGINAL FRAMING'S PREMISE DOES NOT HOLD — four verified facts
**The AFV-F4 commission asserted *"the repository is the PKS domain"* with four roots in and `./architecture/` excluded as *"legacy brainstorming, ungoverned"*.**

1. ⛔ **`engineering/governance/ES-005-Repository.md` EXISTS. §ES-005.1 — *The Three-Concern Separation (EM-001, **ARB 2026-07-10**)* — reads: `docs/` + **`architecture/`** + `app/` + `tests/` = PRODUCT.** ***`architecture/` is explicitly INSIDE the Product concern under an ARB-ruled rule; the exclusion claim CONTRADICTS it.***
2. **ES-005 declares it *"supersedes README/MEMORY as the rule homes for the folder rule"*** — a rule home already exists; a determination ignoring it would amend it by implication.
3. **`.claude/CLAUDE.md`: *"`architecture/` = **Think**"*** — a ROLE, not an exclusion. ***"Legacy during transition" ≠ "ungoverned".***
4. **≥12 top-level roots** (app · architecture · bin · bootstrap · business_analysis · claude · config · database · developer_guide · developer_issues · docs · engineering), and **`architecture/` alone holds 465 markdown files.** *The four-root framing omits ≥7 roots and calls a 465-file tree residue.*

> ***The AFV-F4 repository claim contradicts an ARB-ruled standard, omits most of the repository, and mischaracterizes a governed folder. The earlier refusal to act on it is VINDICATED — a disposition resting on that framing would have amended ES-005.1 BY IMPLICATION.***

### The commission's BINDING FIRST TASK — not a fresh determination
> **Does ES-005.1 GOVERN repository scope today?**

*Tension the assessment must reconcile: **ES-005's document status is PROPOSED**, but **ES-005.1 cites "(EM-001, ARB 2026-07-10)"** — the RULE carries an ARB ruling even where the DOCUMENT does not.*

**Three outcomes: (A) ES-005.1 governs → scope is ALREADY DETERMINED and the AFV-F4 framing was simply wrong · (B) rule ARB-ruled but document unadopted → the question is ES-005's ADOPTION, a different act on a different path · (C) neither governs → only then is a fresh determination in scope.**

***The ordering is binding: a determination made before settling this would either DUPLICATE a governed rule or AMEND it silently.***

### Constraints written in
**No embedded factual assertions** (§1's facts are cited with sources and may be overturned) · **⛔ must NOT retro-ground AFV-F4** — *that would be a RATIONALE change to a discharged act, itself a governance change* · **⛔ NO cleanup authorized, and the proposed cleanup would potentially VIOLATE ES-005.1** · promoted artifacts reachable only by change control · **asymmetry to be VERIFIED, not asserted.**

**Deliverables include a statement on EVERY top-level root, so the four-root framing is not inherited silently.**

**`docs/implementation/PKS_Repository_Scope_Determination.md`.** Executed under the Authority's **four-stage sequence** and **three-layer separation**, with the governing question taken in its refined form — ***"what is the currently binding source governing repository scope, if any?"*** — so no candidate was presumed.

### STAGE 1 — TWO sources bear on repository scope, and they DIFFER
| Source | Standing | Governs |
|---|---|---|
| **R-37 *"Architecture Freeze 2.0 (structural)"*, ARB 2026-07-10**, in `ADR-AIP-LOG-Platform-Rulings.md` | ✅ **BINDING — adopted ruling** | ***"no new top-level folders · no new domain objects"***, issued **at EM-001 closure** |
| **ES-005.1 *The Three-Concern Separation*** | ⚠️ **in a PROPOSED document, with EXISTING executing instruments** | `docs/` + **`architecture/`** + `app/` + `tests/` = **Product** |

### STAGE 2 — the three layers, kept apart
**DOCUMENT:** ES-005 is **PROPOSED** (STANDARDS_INDEX likewise). **RULE:** an ARB act of 2026-07-10 **exists** — R-37. **BINDING EFFECT:** **partly, by TWO DIFFERENT MECHANISMS** — R-37 binds as an adopted ruling; ES-005.1's classification is **operationally enforced** (*STANDARDS_INDEX records its instruments as "Existing (executed each OQ)"*).

### ⚠️ §2.1 — what is NOT established, stated because the temptation is to claim more
> **It is NOT established that the ARB adopted the three-concern separation's TEXT as a rule.**

**R-37's recorded content is a *structural freeze*; ES-005.1's is a *classification*. Adjacent, same date, same migration — but *"issued at EM-001 closure"* is not *"adopted the three-concern text."*** ***What binds is R-37's freeze plus a classification recorded in an unadopted document and enforced by existing instruments. Naming that standing precisely is the determination's main work.***

### STAGES 3–4 — OUTCOME B
**No fresh determination is required. The operative act is ES-005's ADOPTION.**
> ***A fresh determination would either DUPLICATE R-37 and ES-005.1 or AMEND them silently. Neither is neutral.***

### The original AFV-F4 claim — CONTRADICTED on three counts
*`architecture/` excluded* ⛔ ES-005.1 places it INSIDE Product, enforced each OQ · *ungoverned* ⛔ CLAUDE.md assigns it the **"Think"** role; 465 markdown files · *repository = four roots* ⛔ at least twelve exist · *archive the tree* ⛔ **NOT authorized**. **⚠️ On R-37: the freeze forbids *NEW* top-level folders; whether it forbids REMOVING one is NOT established by its text and is expressly left undecided.**

ES-005's adoption · the three-concern TEXT's adoption · **the eight roots ES-005.1 does not classify** (`bin` · `bootstrap` · `business_analysis` · `claude` · `config` · `database` · `developer_guide` · `developer_issues`) · **AFV-F4's rationale — UNTOUCHED, since re-grounding it would be a rationale change to a discharged act** · OQ-PKS-7 · CBC-3 · promotion state · any methodology change.

**Determining scope broadly would classify eight roots no governed source classifies — reaching matters ES-005's adoption should settle.** ***So the NARROW act was to determine the SOURCE and stop; determining the SCOPE would have been the BROAD one.***

## ⛔ "Operationally enforced" CORRECTED — four evidence strengths applied (2026-07-31)

**The Authority's four-tier ladder made me test a claim I had asserted on an index row: *"ES-005.1's classification is OPERATIONALLY ENFORCED."* It compressed four distinct strengths and overstated at least two.**

| Strength | R-37 | ES-005.1 |
|---|---|---|
| **RECORDED** *(somebody wrote it)* | ✅ rulings log | ✅ in ES-005 |
| **REFERENCED** *(later artifacts rely on it)* | ✅ complements R-27 | ✅ STANDARDS_INDEX §54 assigns instruments; **OQ-ENG-002 §50 cites the "three-concern model" in a PASS row** |
| **IMPLEMENTED** *(execution follows it)* | not separately tested | ⚠️ **PARTIALLY** — used as a criterion in an EXECUTED qualification (OQ-ENG-002, 2026-07-11), **but that row checks README's entry-point clarity, NOT the folder classification; and only TWO OQ-ENG executions exist (07-10, 07-11), none since** |
| **BINDING** *(Authority adopted it)* | ✅ **adopted ARB ruling** | ⛔ **NOT DEMONSTRATED** — ES-005 PROPOSED; the three-concern text's adoption unestablished |

> ***My phrase asserted implementation and implied binding force from a single index row. Corrected: RECORDED and REFERENCED with certainty · PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED on one dated execution · NOT DEMONSTRATED as binding.***

**⚠️ Further precision: STANDARDS_INDEX says the instruments are *"executed each OQ"* — true, and there has been NO OQ since 2026-07-11.** ***The enforcement is real but DORMANT; "executed each OQ" reads as ongoing when the last execution is three weeks old.***

### Three Authority insights recorded (Integrity Model)
**1. FOUR STRENGTHS of evidence that a rule governs** — recorded · referenced · implemented · binding. ***Increasing strengths that must not be compressed. A rule can be recorded and referenced without being implemented; implemented without being binding; and binding without any instrument having run recently.***

**2. ⭐ SCOPE vs CLASSIFICATION are DIFFERENT GOVERNANCE OBJECTS:** **R-37 governs STRUCTURE** (*what may exist*) · **ES-005.1 governs MEANING** (*what existing things ARE*). ***Same date, same migration, adjacent artifacts — and conflating them is how "the repository is the PKS domain" came to be asserted as though a structural freeze had settled a classification question.***

**3. THREE kinds of "already governed":** already **DISPOSED** (AFV-F4) · already **PARTIALLY governed** (repository scope) · already **REVIEWED but no longer reproducible** (IBC-1). ***Three different constitutional situations, all requiring the same first question: does governance already exist here?*** **NOT elevated into methodology — recorded as a characteristic of this programme, not a required stage.**

**STATE: repository-scope determination CLOSED at Outcome B with evidence strengths corrected · MCA-R2 commissioned and unexecuted · sixteen artifacts reviewed · no review open · no finding open · register 10.**

**Superseded, retained:** ~~*"the enforcement is real but DORMANT"*~~
**Operative (Authority formulation):** ***implementation evidence exists from prior executed qualifications, but no recent executions have exercised this rule.***

> ***The word "enforcement" smuggles back the BINDING implication the four-strength ladder had just removed. My correction of "operationally enforced" reintroduced "enforcement" one sentence later — the collapse is easy to repeat while explicitly guarding against it.***

**Terminology caution attached to the ladder in the Integrity Model: avoid "enforced"/"enforcement" when reporting IMPLEMENTATION evidence.**

## VERIFICATION USUALLY NARROWS — recorded across five cases (Authority observation)
> **In repository scope · AFV-F4 · M7-F1 · IBC-1 · ES-005.1, the result of additional verification was NOT a stronger conclusion but a MORE PRECISELY BOUNDED one.**

**The discipline it rests on: (1) avoid collapsing different kinds of evidence into a single stronger claim · (2) separate factual observations from governance conclusions · (3) ***increase precision even when it WEAKENS the original statement.***

***Accuracy taking precedence over rhetorical certainty is the property. Five cases are encouraging evidence, not a demonstration that verification always narrows — held provisionally, like every other explanatory model here.***

## 🏁 MCA-R2 EXECUTED — PMR-8 insufficient · PMR-9 CONFIRMED · PMR-10 CONFIRMED (2026-07-31)

**`docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_MCA_R2_Assessment.md`.** Two Authority wording refinements applied first: *"execution evidence shows AT LEAST ONE implemented use… does NOT establish CONTINUOUS OR GENERAL implementation"* (bounds the extent) and the five-case pattern restated as a **tendency**, not a law.

### §1 — THE CENTRAL QUESTION SETTLED FIRST, as a READING of the existing filter
> **ESCAPE means the defect LEFT THE STAGE THAT OWNED THE CHECK** — grounded in RET-1 §5.3's operative sense (*"defects later stages found"*). **A defect PREVENTED, or CAUGHT WITHIN the act that produced it, is NOT an escape.**

*Offered as interpretation, not mandate (CDR-R1 §1's boundary); PMR-8 was not applied to PMR-8.*

### ⭐ §1.1 — the procedure-taint RESIDUAL resolves as a CONSEQUENCE, not separately
| | |
|---|---|
| evidence **PRODUCED BY** the practice | ***yields counterfactuals*** — the practice was applied, so nothing escaped |
| evidence of **ESCAPE** | ***is procedure-INDEPENDENT*** — an escape is an observation of FAILURE, and failures are not products of the practice under assessment |

> ***The taint concern applies to exactly the candidates whose evidence is counterfactual and not at all to those with escapes. The residual is the same distinction seen from the other side.***

### Verdicts
- **PMR-8 — INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE. n=0 escapes.** *Its sole datum is MCA-R1, where the step WAS performed and false rejections were PREVENTED.* ***Structurally self-defeating in the exact way PMR-1's was: PMR-1 failed because routing was "consistent"; PMR-8 fails because the step was PERFORMED. A candidate whose only datum is its own successful voluntary application cannot demonstrate escape.*** **Discharge (a DATA condition): one instance where absent applicability let a false verdict ESCAPE into a decision.**
- **PMR-9 — ✅ CONFIRMED on THREE escapes:** CON-1 (the *"new obligation"* ontology error, caught by the **Authority**) · M7-CF2 (*"strengthened"* and *"class change"*, both caught by the Authority) · IBC-1 re-certification (IBC-M4 graded *"strengthened"*, caught later by the ladder). **MC-1 EXCLUDED — self-caught within the act, not an escape.**
- **PMR-10 — ✅ CONFIRMED on TWO escapes:** the **AFV** collision (blocked an Authority recommendation across three legitimate readings) · the **MCA** collision (**published a false claim that stood three days**). ***The second is one of the strongest individual data points currently in the register: the collision was minted while the programme was actively recording that names cannot be withdrawn — knowing the cost demonstrably did not produce compliance, because the identifier check was NOT AN EXPLICIT REQUIRED STEP, allowing the collision to occur despite awareness of the underlying risk — which is precisely what a control changes.***

### ⚠️ THE REGISTER'S COUNT RE-DERIVED AGAINST THE CRITERIA
**PMR-9's recorded n=2 was INCONSISTENT WITH THE ASSESSMENT CRITERIA** — it counted MC-1 (not an escape) and omitted two escapes. ***The undercount is itself a datum for the candidate: the register recorded instances by NOTABILITY rather than by the filter's own test — which is what happens when classification precedes verification.***

### Boundaries
Nothing adopted · PMR-8 not applied to PMR-8 (the filter was READ, and all three are controls so no applicability adaptation was needed) · **counts NOT aggregated** · the filter's blind spot (*it measures only defect prevention*) **carried forward UNRESOLVED** · no new candidate; register stays at 10 · SDM v1.1 / EOP v1.1 operative.

**FOR THE CDR, not decided here:** adopt PMR-9? · adopt PMR-10? · does PMR-8's narrower value warrant adoption despite failing an escape-based filter? · **should the filter's blind spot itself be revisited?**

**STATE: MCA-R2 CLOSED · PMR-9 and PMR-10 CONFIRMED awaiting CDR · PMR-8 insufficient · sixteen artifacts reviewed · no review open · no finding open · register 10.**

## MCA-R2 — four Authority wording refinements applied (2026-07-31)

**1. Superlative bounded:** ~~*"the strongest single datum in the register"*~~ → ***"one of the strongest individual data points currently in the register."*** *Ranking evidence is harder than identifying it, and the programme has no explicit weighting framework — so a superlative asserted a comparison it could not perform.*

**2. Interpretive phrase replaced with observable evidence:** ~~*"the check was a HABIT rather than a STEP"*~~ → ***"the identifier check was NOT AN EXPLICIT REQUIRED STEP, allowing the collision to occur despite awareness of the underlying risk."***

**3. Register characterization corrected:** ~~*"the register's n=2 was WRONG IN COMPOSITION and LOW"*~~ → ***"the register's counts were INCONSISTENT WITH THE ASSESSMENT CRITERIA."*** ***The EVIDENCE did not change; its CLASSIFICATION against the filter did.*** *This is governance improving its own bookkeeping, not revising an evidence base.*

**4. PMR-8's outcome stated precisely:**
> ⚠️ **This does NOT show that PMR-8 lacks value. It shows that THE CURRENT ADMISSION CRITERION — escape evidence — HAS NOT BEEN SATISFIED.**

***The candidate may still have preventive value; it has simply not met the programme's current threshold for admission. Those are different findings, and only the second is within an assessment's authority to make.***

**All three sets of counts, verdicts and evidence are unchanged by these refinements — only the characterizations were tightened.**

**STATE: MCA-R2 CLOSED · PMR-9 and PMR-10 CONFIRMED awaiting a CDR-class decision · PMR-8 insufficient under the current criterion · sixteen artifacts reviewed · no review open · no finding open · register 10.**

## 🏁 CDR-R2 ISSUED — PMR-9 ADOPT · PMR-10 ADOPT · PMR-8 DEFER (2026-07-31)

**`docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_CDR_R2_Decision.md`. Baseline NOT amended — decision ≠ execution.**

**⚠️ Scope note recorded: the instruction line named PMR-9 and PMR-10; the same message's body named all three. All three decided, since an assessed candidate left undecided is an open item — and DEFER is the only disposition consistent with PMR-8's assessment.**

### PMR-9 — ✅ ADOPT, verbatim
> **Where a finding's CLASS depends on what a governing source actually says, the source shall be verified before the finding is classified — not merely before it is asserted.**

***Why the evidence is HETEROGENEOUS rather than REPETITIVE: the three escapes occurred at different times, on different artifacts, and produced DIFFERENT ERROR TYPES — an ontology error (CON-1), a severity error (M7-CF2), a grading error (IBC-M4). That is more convincing than three repetitions of one mistake, because a single recurring slip could be idiosyncratic while three distinct failure shapes indicate a MISSING STEP.***

### PMR-10 — ✅ ADOPT, verbatim
> **Before an identifier or acronym is minted, the corpus shall be searched for existing use of that token. Where the token is taken, a distinct one is chosen.**

**⚠️ Prospective scope matters more here than for PMR-9:** ***the two existing collisions CANNOT be cured by this rule — identifier stability forbids renaming, so they are bounded, never erased. Adoption prevents the THIRD collision; it does not repair the first two.*** **Renaming `AFV-1` / `MCA-R1` remains FORBIDDEN; no retroactive collision audit is authorized; the AFV normalization stays on the backlog by ALIAS only.**

**Neither rule improved during adoption** — *the Assembly Fidelity Rule is GOVERNED and ISV-F1 established that paraphrasing while asserting verbatim breaches it at the moment of enactment.*

### PMR-8 — ⏸ DEFER, not decline
> ⚠️ **The assessment showed THE CURRENT ADMISSION CRITERION was unsatisfied, not that the candidate lacks value.** ***Declining would answer a question the assessment expressly did not answer.***

**⚠️ Structural difficulty recorded rather than glossed: PMR-8's discharge requires a governance FAILURE the programme is actively trying to prevent.** *The condition is satisfiable only by the control's absence causing harm — hardest to obtain for candidates already practised voluntarily. Whether that is a correct property of the filter or a defect in it is the blind-spot question.*

### NOT decided
**The escape filter's BLIND SPOT** *(measures only defect prevention — revising it would amend the framework's ADMISSION LADDER, not a PMR, and no candidate for it is before the CDR)* **· an alternative admission path for PMR-8 · the AFV normalization · any CDR-R1 entry · certification state (Operational Evidence still zero-independent).**

### Admission discipline held
Nothing adopted on insufficient evidence (PMR-8 deferred, not rescued) · nothing adopted on the basis of PREFERENCE rather than EVIDENCE · **no wording improved during adoption** · no retroactive application · ***the filter was NOT revised to admit a candidate — the blind spot was left open rather than resolved in a direction that would have helped PMR-8.***

**Arithmetic: three candidates · **two adopted WITHOUT AMENDMENT** · one deferred on a data condition — and the two adopted are the only two that showed escapes.**

**NEXT: one controlled-change act — issue the amended baseline carrying PMR-9 and PMR-10 verbatim.**

**STATE: CDR-R2 ISSUED · SDM v1.1 / EOP v1.1 operative pending baseline issuance · sixteen artifacts reviewed · no review open · no finding open · register 10.**

## 🏁 SDM v1.2 / EOP v1.2 ISSUED — PMR-9 and PMR-10 now GOVERNED (2026-07-31)

**`docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_Methodology_Baseline_v1_2.md`. OPERATIVE.** Three Authority wording refinements applied first: **"two adopted WITHOUT AMENDMENT"** (matching the Assembly Fidelity Rule's own language) · **"nothing adopted on the basis of PREFERENCE rather than EVIDENCE"** · **"HETEROGENEOUS rather than REPETITIVE"** evidence (no implicit ranking).

### ⚠️ §1 — VERBATIM VERIFICATION PERFORMED BEFORE THE CLAIM WAS MADE
**Both clauses were extracted MECHANICALLY from the register and carried unmodified — 174 and 156 characters — then DIFFED post-issuance: PMR-9 IDENTICAL · PMR-10 IDENTICAL.**

> ***ISV-F1 established that asserting verbatim reproduction while carrying a paraphrase breaches the Assembly Fidelity Rule at the moment of enacting it. The previous issuance made the claim without performing the check; the check is the only thing distinguishing a verbatim claim from a hopeful one.***

### The two rules now GOVERNED, prospective
- **PMR-9:** *"Where a finding's CLASS depends on what a governing source actually says, the source shall be verified before the finding is classified — not merely before it is asserted."* Evidence heterogeneous rather than repetitive (ontology · severity · grading errors).
- **PMR-10:** *"Before an identifier or acronym is minted, the corpus shall be searched for existing use of that token. Where the token is taken, a distinct one is chosen."*
  **⚠️ Non-curability carried INTO the baseline: *the two collisions that evidenced this rule CANNOT be cured by it — identifier stability forbids renaming, so AFV-1 and MCA-R1 are bounded, never erased. It prevents the THIRD collision; it does not repair the first two.*** Renaming remains FORBIDDEN; no retroactive collision audit authorized.

### Unchanged
All v1.1 adoptions (Assembly Fidelity Rule · Strategic/Tactical core-principle status with its no-precedence exclusion · two-level determinism vocabulary with its CI-1 constraint) · MCR-1..6 · **the escape filter and admission ladder, with the blind spot LEFT OPEN** · certification state (Operational Evidence still zero-independent) · **v1.1 not edited or retracted — forward-only** · **extraction/consolidation still AUTHORIZED and still UNCOMMISSIONED.**

### No immediate implementation act follows
***Both rules are prospective and self-executing: they constrain future assessments and future identifier minting, and require no editorial act against any existing artifact.***

## ES-005-ADOPT-1 — two refinements applied · two principles recorded (2026-07-31)

### 1. Implementation status no longer attributed UNIFORMLY
**Superseded:** ~~*"already recorded, referenced and implemented at least once"*~~
**Operative:** ***"already recorded and referenced, with evidence of at least one implementation use WHERE SEPARATELY DEMONSTRATED."***
*Implementation evidence was demonstrated for the **three-concern classification only** — the decision must not carry it across to the folder rule or the placement litmus without their own evidence.* **My phrasing would have attributed implementation to all three ARB-cited rules on evidence for one.**

### 2. The eight-roots concern stated more precisely
**Superseded:** ~~*"adoption would FREEZE that gap into a governed rule"*~~
**Operative:** ***"adopting a repository-classification rule without explicitly stating the status of the remaining top-level roots risks unintentionally implying that the governed classification is EXHAUSTIVE."***
***The narrower claim is the stronger one: the risk is not that the roots become frozen but that READERS MAY REASONABLY INFER COMPLETENESS where none was decided.***

### ⭐ 3. ADOPTION CHANGES BINDING STATUS, NOT CONTENT (Integrity Model — governance vocabulary)
| Act | Changes |
|---|---|
| **EDITING** a rule | its **content** *(own authority required; forbidden during adoption)* |
| **ADOPTING** a rule | its **binding status** — hence its **enforceability**, not current practice |
| **IMPLEMENTING** a rule | whether **execution follows** it |

***A rule can be implemented before it is adopted, and adopted without any change in behaviour. Adoption is about ENFORCEABILITY.***

### ⭐ 4. THE GOVERNANCE UNIT SHOULD MATCH THE AUTHORITY UNIT (Integrity Model)
**Six instances: findings → the individual PROPOSITION (CON-F2→M7-F1) · PMRs → the individual CANDIDATE (PMR-7 split) · ES-005 → the individual RULE, not the document · M7 → the individual CHANGE ITEM (C-M7-01) · SDM/EOP → the BASELINE ISSUANCE · CDR → the individual DISPOSITION.**

> ***Avoid bundling decisions whose EVIDENCE, AUTHORITY or MATURITY differ. Where they differ and are bundled, the strongest element carries the weakest — which is the composite-confidence defect (PMR-2) operating on GOVERNANCE OBJECTS rather than on CLAIMS.***

**Held as an observed architectural direction, not admitted as a rule.**

### Noted on the two rules exercised
**The Authority's framing adopted: PMR-10's value is not *"no collision occurred"* but that ***the collision check was EXPLICITLY PERFORMED before minting*** — an explicit governance step rather than an implicit habit. Likewise PMR-9's value is not that the citations agreed but that ***classification followed VERIFICATION rather than RECOLLECTION.***

## Bundling MECHANISM stated · ES-005-ADOPT-1 readiness confirmed but NOT self-authorized (2026-07-31)

### The bundling mechanism, explained rather than described
**Superseded, retained:** ~~*"the strongest element carries the weakest"*~~ — *described the OUTCOME without the mechanism, and asserted that every bundle fails.*
**Operative (Authority formulation):**
> **When governance objects with different evidence, authority, or maturity are bundled into a single decision, THE BUNDLE SHARES ONE AUTHORITY BOUNDARY WHILE ITS EVIDENCE REMAINS UNEVEN. That can allow weaker elements to acquire governance status they have not independently justified.**

***Ties directly to the composite-confidence reasoning (PMR-2) without assuming inevitability: bundling CAN allow unjustified elevation, and whether it does depends on the bundle.***

### ⚠️ READINESS IS NOT AUTHORIZATION — the commission is NOT executed
**The Authority's message concludes that ES-005-ADOPT-1 *"is now ready for execution"* and lists the properties supporting that. It does not instruct execution.**

> ***Readiness is a DERIVED STATE, not a DECISION — the same distinction the promotion chain already carries ("Promotion Ready is a derived state, not a decision"; verified-ready ≠ promoted). Executing on a readiness finding would collapse the two.***

**ES-005-ADOPT-1 therefore stands COMMISSIONED, REFINED, and READY — awaiting an explicit instruction to execute.**

**The readiness properties the Authority verified, recorded:** evidence verified before recommendation · per-rule rather than wholesale adoption · no embedded assumptions about binding status · no propagation of implementation evidence · no unintended expansion of repository classification · clear separation between content, adoption and implementation.

**The commission said *"eight unclassified roots."* Wrong — it came from a TRUNCATED listing.**
**24 top-level directories · 6 classified by ES-005.1 (`docs` · `architecture` · `app` · `tests` · `engineering` · `.claude`) · **18 UNCLASSIFIED**.**

- **ES-005.1 — ✅ ADOPT, with a NON-EXHAUSTIVENESS RIDER forming part of the adoption:** *the classification is NOT exhaustive — 6 of 24 roots; the remaining 18 are unclassified and this adoption classifies none of them.* Rule ARB-ruled · recorded · referenced · **one verified implementation use** (OQ-ENG-002 §50) · **now BINDING**.
